- **h-compare**: Compare two or more existing candidates under an explicit characteristic space, parity basis, and predeclared selection policy. Return constraints, trade-offs, and a non-dominated set rather than hiding the choice in one score. This skill is independent of h-frame and h-explore. Default to a conversational comparison; persist comparison artifacts only on explicit save intent or when current Work supplies a concrete operator-named or agent-inferred receiving use that needs replay. A binding choice requires a direct unambiguous operator request routed through h-decide. ([asermax/claude-plugins](https://github.com/asermax/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/haft/skills/h-compare) · ★3 · 2 copies)
